# 15 - Volume-by-Volume Evidence Ledger

## 0. Role and authority

This document is the Phase-5 chronological spine for specialist synthesis. It does not supersede the interpretive prose of the canonical V2 deep readings; it **does supersede earlier paragraph-coordinate forms wherever the Phase-5 correction ledger records a changed locator**. The original deep-reading packages remain frozen provenance objects and are not silently rewritten.

- Mainline volumes locked: **14/14**
- Canonical deep-reading locators locked: **1045/1045**
- Locator corrections/backfills preserved in crosswalk: **88**
- T14 claim-audit boundaries: **41**
- T14 questions preserved OPEN: **32**
- Locked Japanese EPUB integrity: **16/16**

### Phase-5 locator rule

When a locator appears in `86_PHASE5_LOCATOR_CORRECTION_LEDGER.tsv`, later documents must use the `locked_locator` and `locked_anchor` from the Phase-5 index. The correction changes coordinates or transcription only unless a later artifact explicitly records an interpretive revision.

# V01 - 86─エイティシックス─

**Phase-5 volume thesis:** Administrative and linguistic dehumanization makes physical humans cease to count legally, statistically, and archivally; names function as counter-archive and accountability, while Lena still lacks particular knowledge and Shin has almost no usable future horizon.

**Mandatory revision boundary:** Do not reduce Lena arc to learning that the Eighty-Six are people; do not treat pride as latent pathology.

## Source structure

- 序章　戦野に紅く雛罌粟の咲く
- 第一章　戦死者ゼロの戦場
- 第二章　白骨戦線異状なし
- 第三章　夜闇の冥府のほとりにおけるご立派な君の
- 間章　首のない騎士
- 第四章　我が名は〈亡霊の軍勢〉、数多なれば
- 間章　首のない騎士Ⅱ
- 第五章　スピアヘッド戦隊にクソ栄光あれ
- 間章　首のない騎士Ⅲ
- 第六章　せめて人間たらんと
- 間章　首のない騎士Ⅳ
- 第七章　さよなら
- 終章　鮮血女王のお成り
- 終章─二　Ｒｅｂｏｏｔ──始動
- あとがき

## Evidence-responsibility checklist

- **Decisive events / causal reconstruction:** governed by the canonical V2 deep reading and the locked locator table below.
- **Character and relationship changes:** route to L01/L02 and T06/T09 where ordinary-life and relationship evidence is needed.
- **Institutions / politics:** route to L03 and T10/T11.
- **Military:** route claims through the Phase-5 military-level lock before specialist reuse.
- **Personhood / technology:** route to L05 and T02/T07.
- **Ethics / responsibility:** route through T08 plus the T14 claim-counterevidence lock.
- **Japanese voice / terminology:** T12 constraints are mandatory; English master terms must not erase lexical distinctions.
- **Motifs / visual form:** route to T13 and preserve the visual-evidence layer.
- **Later revisions:** use T14 plus the volume-specific revision boundary above.

## Relevant V14-boundary open questions

- **T14-OQ-07 - Postwar reparative order:** What compensation, archive, education, reconstruction, and legal responsibility follow Republic/Federacy wrongdoing?
- **T14-OQ-08 - Race/caste after V14:** How will final-arc politics distinguish race, ethnicity, caste, aristocratic blood legitimacy, and military status?
- **T14-OQ-32 - Eighty-Six identity after war:** Does Eighty-Six remain a chosen durable identity across citizenship, locality, family, and generational change?

# V02 - 86―エイティシックス―Ep.2 ―ラン・スルー・ザ・バトルフロント―〈上

**Phase-5 volume thesis:** Rescue does not erase identity, and identity does not settle destiny. Real alternatives make reenlistment meaningful without making it historically unconditioned; paternalistic recovery scripts are distinguished from capability-building scaffolding.

**Mandatory revision boundary:** Do not call Giad merely a nicer Republic; do not call military choice inauthentic solely because trauma shaped it.

## Source structure

- 序章　女王陛下は戦都にいまし
- 第一章　ワルキューレの騎行
- 第二章　パンツァー・リート
- 第三章　ワイルド・ブルー・ヤンダー
- 第四章　双頭の鷲の旗の下に
- 第五章　クライズ・テイク・エイム
- 間章　ウェン・〝ジョン・ドゥ〟・カムズ・マーチング・ホーム
- あとがき

# V03 - 86─エイティシックス─Ep.3 ─ラン・スルー・ザ・バトルフロント─〈下〉

**Phase-5 volume thesis:** Bare survival is not yet a life. Pride can preserve dignity yet become enclosing when it is the only admissible identity; dangerous military necessity must remain distinct from disposal, and return becomes an explicit operational and relational expectation.

**Mandatory revision boundary:** Do not collapse survival preference into positive life plan; do not equate dangerous mission with disposal.

## Source structure

- 間章　ゲット・ユア・ガンズ
- 第六章　オーバー・ゼア
- 第七章　死して甲斐あるものなれば
- 第八章　ラン・スルー・ザ・バトルフロント
- 第九章　久しく待ちにし
- 終章　ウィル・ミート・アゲイン
- あとがき

- **T14-OQ-06 - Return capability:** Can retreat and physical return become protected institutional expectations rather than privileges that disappear under crisis?
- **T14-OQ-18 - Undertaker after war:** Can Undertaker remain historical identity without continuing necessity reproducing the office?
- **T14-OQ-28 - Military vocation:** How much of continued soldierhood after rescue is chosen vocation, gravitational habit, conditioned identity, or practical wartime necessity?

# V04 - 86─エイティシックス─Ep.4 ─アンダー・プレッシャー─

**Phase-5 volume thesis:** Correctly perceiving another wound does not grant ownership over its meaning. Phonix complicates simple personhood shortcuts, while command succession and the Republic as an internalized world-model remain active concerns.

**Mandatory revision boundary:** Preserve epistemic boundary: insight does not create interpretive ownership.

## Source structure

- 序章　ミッシング・イン・アクション
- 第一章　コール・オン・デューティ
- 第二章　アイデンティフィケーション・フレンド・オア・フォー
- 第三章　フロント・トゥワード・エネミー
- 第四章　トリアージ
- 終章　ウーンデッド・イン・アクション
- あとがき

## Evidence-responsibility checklist

- **Decisive events / causal reconstruction:** governed by the canonical V2 deep reading and the locked locator table below.
- **Character and relationship changes:** route to L01/L02 and T06/T09 where ordinary-life and relationship evidence is needed.
- **Institutions / politics:** route to L03 and T10/T11.
- **Military:** route claims through the Phase-5 military-level lock before specialist reuse.
- **Personhood / technology:** route to L05 and T02/T07.
- **Ethics / responsibility:** route through T08 plus the T14 claim-counterevidence lock.
- **Japanese voice / terminology:** T12 constraints are mandatory; English master terms must not erase lexical distinctions.
- **Motifs / visual form:** route to T13 and preserve the visual-evidence layer.
- **Later revisions:** use T14 plus the volume-specific revision boundary above.

## Relevant V14-boundary open questions

- **T14-OQ-15 - Phonix individuality:** What evidence would count as autonomous end formation rather than sophisticated adaptive behavior?
- **T14-OQ-20 - Lena interpretive authority:** Can Lena remain compassionate without converting accurate insight into authority over Shin meaning?

# V05 - 86─エイティシックス─Ep.5　─死よ、驕るなかれ─

**Phase-5 volume thesis:** Sirin/Roa cases separate origin, substrate, personality, identity, desire, refusal, embodiment, and moral standing. Open self-revision is not a minimum personhood threshold, and technological continuation is not restoration.

**Mandatory revision boundary:** Do not use open-ended revisability as the threshold for moral standing.

## Source structure

- 序章　屍の王
- 第一章　怪物どもの憂愁
- 第二章　白鳥の砦
- 第三章　シンギング・バードの嘆きも知らず
- 第四章　エクスマキナ
- 終章　花など咲かぬ、雪の野に
- あとがき

## Evidence-responsibility checklist

- **Decisive events / causal reconstruction:** governed by the canonical V2 deep reading and the locked locator table below.
- **Character and relationship changes:** route to L01/L02 and T06/T09 where ordinary-life and relationship evidence is needed.
- **Institutions / politics:** route to L03 and T10/T11.
- **Military:** route claims through the Phase-5 military-level lock before specialist reuse.
- **Personhood / technology:** route to L05 and T02/T07.
- **Ethics / responsibility:** route through T08 plus the T14 claim-counterevidence lock.

## Relevant V14-boundary open questions

- **T14-OQ-10 - Artificial civic standing:** What legal status can Sirins, Fido-like subjects, Zelene, and other artificial subjects possess?
- **T14-OQ-11 - Lyudmila scope:** Is Lyudmila emergent fear an individual relationship effect, a repeatable Sirin capacity, or evidence of broader designed-subject revisability?
- **T14-OQ-14 - Zelene continuity:** How much selfhood and responsibility persist through Zelene Legion existence?
- **T14-OQ-15 - Phonix individuality:** What evidence would count as autonomous end formation rather than sophisticated adaptive behavior?

# V06 - 86─エイティシックス─Ep.6 ─明けねばこそ夜は永く─

**Phase-5 volume thesis:** The Five move from exclusive to non-exclusive identity: pride and military role can remain while relational desire and additional futures become possible. Freedom is not lack of ties but ties that do not exhaust the self.

**Mandatory revision boundary:** Do not read relational attachment as loss of freedom; test whether ties remain non-exhaustive.

## Source structure

- 序章　ハーシュ・ミストレス
- 第一章　人狼は森に
- 第二章　ライフズ・バッド・ア・ウォーキング・シャドウ
- 第三章　シュート・ザ・ムーン
- 第四章　イン・ヒズ・ヘヴン
- 終章　ホーム・スイート・ホーム
- あとがき

## Evidence-responsibility checklist

- **Decisive events / causal reconstruction:** governed by the canonical V2 deep reading and the locked locator table below.
- **Character and relationship changes:** route to L01/L02 and T06/T09 where ordinary-life and relationship evidence is needed.
- **Institutions / politics:** route to L03 and T10/T11.
- **Military:** route claims through the Phase-5 military-level lock before specialist reuse.
- **Personhood / technology:** route to L05 and T02/T07.
- **Ethics / responsibility:** route through T08 plus the T14 claim-counterevidence lock.
- **Japanese voice / terminology:** T12 constraints are mandatory; English master terms must not erase lexical distinctions.
- **Motifs / visual form:** route to T13 and preserve the visual-evidence layer.
- **Later revisions:** use T14 plus the volume-specific revision boundary above.

## Relevant V14-boundary open questions

- **T14-OQ-17 - Shin postwar vocation:** Which nonmilitary preferences become durable when war no longer supplies the next task?
- **T14-OQ-27 - Group divergence:** How far can Spearhead lives diverge while the group remains a home rather than a role-enforcing identity?

# V07 - 86─エイティシックス─Ep.7 ─ミスト─

**Phase-5 volume thesis:** Ordinary-life capability expands through school, leave, dance, travel, etiquette, and confession. Anti-hero doctrine argues for organizational redundancy while Shin articulates a positive shared future with Lena.

**Mandatory revision boundary:** Do not read anti-hero doctrine as hostility to excellence; distinguish person particularity from function redundancy.

## Source structure

- 序章　戦場の霧
- 第一章　ヘイズ・ブルー
- 第二章　ミスト・ブルー
- 第三章　フォグ・ブルー
- 第四章　スターライト・ブルー
- あとがき

## Evidence-responsibility checklist

- **Decisive events / causal reconstruction:** governed by the canonical V2 deep reading and the locked locator table below.
- **Character and relationship changes:** route to L01/L02 and T06/T09 where ordinary-life and relationship evidence is needed.
- **Institutions / politics:** route to L03 and T10/T11.
- **Military:** route claims through the Phase-5 military-level lock before specialist reuse.
- **Personhood / technology:** route to L05 and T02/T07.
- **Ethics / responsibility:** route through T08 plus the T14 claim-counterevidence lock.
- **Japanese voice / terminology:** T12 constraints are mandatory; English master terms must not erase lexical distinctions.
- **Motifs / visual form:** route to T13 and preserve the visual-evidence layer.
- **Later revisions:** use T14 plus the volume-specific revision boundary above.

## Relevant V14-boundary open questions

- **T14-OQ-19 - Lena overresponsibility:** Can bounded responsibility survive direct personal catastrophe without renewed self-spending?
- **T14-OQ-20 - Lena interpretive authority:** Can Lena remain compassionate without converting accurate insight into authority over Shin meaning?
- **T14-OQ-23 - Kurena independent worth:** Can Kurena build durable self-worth not organized around protecting or being useful to loved people?

# V08 - 86─エイティシックス─Ep.8 ─ガンスモーク・オン・ザ・ウォーター─

**Phase-5 volume thesis:** The series tests reconstruction after involuntary loss: Theo loses his old combat body, Shin disappears operationally without collapsing the force, the Fleet Countries lose institutional continuity, and the sea refuses to remain a simple healing symbol.

**Mandatory revision boundary:** Do not call disability automatic liberation; do not treat the sea as one fixed freedom symbol.

## Source structure

- 序章　ザ・レッド・ドラゴン
- 第一章　ザ・ガン・イン・ザ・ハイ・キャッスル
- 第二章　モービィ・ディック・オア・ザ・ホエイル
- 第三章　イントゥ・ザ・ストーム
- 第四章　ザ・タワー（アップライト）
- 第五章　ザ・タワー（リバース）
- あとがき

## Evidence-responsibility checklist

- **Decisive events / causal reconstruction:** governed by the canonical V2 deep reading and the locked locator table below.
- **Character and relationship changes:** route to L01/L02 and T06/T09 where ordinary-life and relationship evidence is needed.
- **Institutions / politics:** route to L03 and T10/T11.
- **Military:** route claims through the Phase-5 military-level lock before specialist reuse.
- **Personhood / technology:** route to L05 and T02/T07.
- **Ethics / responsibility:** route through T08 plus the T14 claim-counterevidence lock.
- **Japanese voice / terminology:** T12 constraints are mandatory; English master terms must not erase lexical distinctions.
- **Motifs / visual form:** route to T13 and preserve the visual-evidence layer.
- **Later revisions:** use T14 plus the volume-specific revision boundary above.

## Relevant V14-boundary open questions

- **T14-OQ-22 - Theo post-disability choice:** Can Theo continue military/technical service without adaptation becoming a new duty to prove usefulness?
- **T14-OQ-27 - Group divergence:** How far can Spearhead lives diverge while the group remains a home rather than a role-enforcing identity?
- **T14-OQ-28 - Military vocation:** How much of continued soldierhood after rescue is chosen vocation, gravitational habit, conditioned identity, or practical wartime necessity?

# V09 - 86─エイティシックス─Ep.9 ─ヴァルキリィ・ハズ・ランデッド─

**Phase-5 volume thesis:** Pride, wish, bond, prayer, and curse become one binding-force problem. Theo begins a post-function future through small desires; Shin/Lena become explicitly reciprocal; the Theocracy tests high-status role capture.

**Mandatory revision boundary:** Do not treat curse as a synonym for bad bond; preserve binding ambiguity and small-desire futurity.

## Source structure

- 序章　暴食の獣
- 第一章　人魚の取引
- 間章　スペードの王とハートの女王の、とても永くてくだらない諍い
- 第二章　灰かぶりの戦場
- 間章　青い鳥はどこにいたのか
- 第三章　彼女の首を刎ねよ
- 間章　ところでジークフリートの殺し方を知るには
- 第四章　鏡よ鏡、ただの鏡に映るのは？
- 第五章　笛吹きはネズミどもと子供を連れて
- 終章　ワニの腹でも時計は進む
- あとがき

## Evidence-responsibility checklist

- **Decisive events / causal reconstruction:** governed by the canonical V2 deep reading and the locked locator table below.
- **Character and relationship changes:** route to L01/L02 and T06/T09 where ordinary-life and relationship evidence is needed.
- **Institutions / politics:** route to L03 and T10/T11.
- **Military:** route claims through the Phase-5 military-level lock before specialist reuse.
- **Personhood / technology:** route to L05 and T02/T07.
- **Ethics / responsibility:** route through T08 plus the T14 claim-counterevidence lock.
- **Japanese voice / terminology:** T12 constraints are mandatory; English master terms must not erase lexical distinctions.
- **Motifs / visual form:** route to T13 and preserve the visual-evidence layer.
- **Later revisions:** use T14 plus the volume-specific revision boundary above.

## Relevant V14-boundary open questions

- **T14-OQ-22 - Theo post-disability choice:** Can Theo continue military/technical service without adaptation becoming a new duty to prove usefulness?
- **T14-OQ-24 - Anju and Dustin:** Can future commitment coexist with Daiya memory without narrating him as a hurdle that must be overcome?
- **T14-OQ-27 - Group divergence:** How far can Spearhead lives diverge while the group remains a home rather than a role-enforcing identity?

# V10 - ８６―エイティシックス―Ep.10 ―フラグメンタル・ネオテニー―

**Phase-5 volume thesis:** Undertaker is revealed as a socially assembled historical self rather than untouched essence. Inherited fragments can be kept, rejected, shared, or reinterpreted without redeeming the violence that supplied them; Fido establishes artificial first-person continuity.

**Mandatory revision boundary:** Do not turn historical construction into falseness or redeem violence because later meaning becomes valuable.

## Source structure

## Evidence-responsibility checklist

- **Decisive events / causal reconstruction:** governed by the canonical V2 deep reading and the locked locator table below.
- **Character and relationship changes:** route to L01/L02 and T06/T09 where ordinary-life and relationship evidence is needed.
- **Institutions / politics:** route to L03 and T10/T11.
- **Military:** route claims through the Phase-5 military-level lock before specialist reuse.
- **Personhood / technology:** route to L05 and T02/T07.
- **Ethics / responsibility:** route through T08 plus the T14 claim-counterevidence lock.
- **Japanese voice / terminology:** T12 constraints are mandatory; English master terms must not erase lexical distinctions.
- **Motifs / visual form:** route to T13 and preserve the visual-evidence layer.
- **Later revisions:** use T14 plus the volume-specific revision boundary above.

## Relevant V14-boundary open questions

- **T14-OQ-10 - Artificial civic standing:** What legal status can Sirins, Fido-like subjects, Zelene, and other artificial subjects possess?
- **T14-OQ-18 - Undertaker after war:** Can Undertaker remain historical identity without continuing necessity reproducing the office?
- **T14-OQ-30 - Becoming after war:** What does becoming mean once crisis no longer forces repeated decisions and identities can stabilize for long periods?

# V11 - ８６―エイティシックス―Ep.11 ―ディエス・パシオニス―

**Phase-5 volume thesis:** Nonrevenge is separated from forgiveness; grief from guilt; rescue from affection; national origin from home; and inability to save from culpability. Logistics and rotating withdrawal show how institutions materialize these distinctions.

**Mandatory revision boundary:** Nonrevenge is not forgiveness; grief is not guilt; rescue does not certify affection.

- **Military:** route claims through the Phase-5 military-level lock before specialist reuse.

## Relevant V14-boundary open questions

- **T14-OQ-07 - Postwar reparative order:** What compensation, archive, education, reconstruction, and legal responsibility follow Republic/Federacy wrongdoing?
- **T14-OQ-19 - Lena overresponsibility:** Can bounded responsibility survive direct personal catastrophe without renewed self-spending?
- **T14-OQ-26 - Rito public memory:** Can public vindication preserve Rito particularity instead of converting him into a clean martyr-symbol?

# V12 - ８６―エイティシックス―Ep.12　─ホーリィ・ブルー・ブレット─

**Phase-5 volume thesis:** Formal freedom becomes usable only through education, practice, material support, recoverable error, and relationships that help without deciding permanently. Hail Mary supplies the negative model of outsourced judgment and charismatic capture.

**Mandatory revision boundary:** Scaffolding enables but does not guarantee self-government; fallibility cannot justify permanent guardianship.

## Source structure

- 序章　メアリィ・ブルーの聖地
- 第一章　優しく美しきクイーン・メアリィの、美しく優しいはずだった世界
- 第二章　メアリィ・スーの行進
- 第三章　叶いたまえ、ヘイル・メアリィ
- 第四章　メアリィの小さな仔羊と、いつもの羊狩り骸骨たち
- 第五章　ブラッディ・メアリィは霧の中
- 終章　メアリィ・ジェーンの悪夢へようこそ、親愛なる鹿狩人
- あとがき

## Evidence-responsibility checklist

- **Decisive events / causal reconstruction:** governed by the canonical V2 deep reading and the locked locator table below.
- **Character and relationship changes:** route to L01/L02 and T06/T09 where ordinary-life and relationship evidence is needed.
- **Institutions / politics:** route to L03 and T10/T11.
- **Military:** route claims through the Phase-5 military-level lock before specialist reuse.
- **Personhood / technology:** route to L05 and T02/T07.
- **Ethics / responsibility:** route through T08 plus the T14 claim-counterevidence lock.
- **Japanese voice / terminology:** T12 constraints are mandatory; English master terms must not erase lexical distinctions.
- **Motifs / visual form:** route to T13 and preserve the visual-evidence layer.
- **Later revisions:** use T14 plus the volume-specific revision boundary above.

## Relevant V14-boundary open questions

- **T14-OQ-01 - Federacy constitutional survival:** Does the Federacy remain a constitutional order after the reactionary break, rather than merely a surviving territory or military coalition?
- **T14-OQ-03 - Emergency powers:** Which V12-V14 restrictions expire, which are reviewed, and which harden into ordinary rule?
- **T14-OQ-04 - Political recoverability:** Can a polity institutionally return from fear and scapegoating without simply restoring an older regime or relying on one exceptional ruler?

# V13 - ８６―エイティシックス―Ep.13　─ディア・ハンター─

**Phase-5 volume thesis:** The Federacy can reproduce a Sector relation without becoming historically identical to San Magnolia. Fawns establish an anti-disposability floor; fear and propaganda attack social cohesion; interruptibility and return capability become central tests.

**Mandatory revision boundary:** Sector analogy must specify matched mechanisms and unmatched history; recognition of humanity does not prevent disposability.

## Source structure

- 序章　ふりさけみれば
- 第一章　いざ言とはむ
- 第二章　面影は見ゆらむものを
- 第三章　いかが答へし
- 第四章　恨みつべしや
- 第五章　出でしあとの月影
- あとがき

## Evidence-responsibility checklist

- **Decisive events / causal reconstruction:** governed by the canonical V2 deep reading and the locked locator table below.
- **Character and relationship changes:** route to L01/L02 and T06/T09 where ordinary-life and relationship evidence is needed.
- **Institutions / politics:** route to L03 and T10/T11.
- **Military:** route claims through the Phase-5 military-level lock before specialist reuse.
- **Personhood / technology:** route to L05 and T02/T07.
- **Ethics / responsibility:** route through T08 plus the T14 claim-counterevidence lock.
- **Japanese voice / terminology:** T12 constraints are mandatory; English master terms must not erase lexical distinctions.
- **Motifs / visual form:** route to T13 and preserve the visual-evidence layer.
- **Later revisions:** use T14 plus the volume-specific revision boundary above.

## Relevant V14-boundary open questions

- **T14-OQ-06 - Return capability:** Can retreat and physical return become protected institutional expectations rather than privileges that disappear under crisis?
- **T14-OQ-09 - Fawn future:** Can Fawns be medically stabilized and civically integrated without either denial of danger or detention-as-disposal?
- **T14-OQ-21 - Raiden own future:** What does Raiden want beyond stabilizing, commanding, and returning others?
- **T14-OQ-25 - Frederica self-rule:** What does being her own king mean once inherited monarchy becomes politically active around her body?

# V14 - ８６―エイティシックス―Ep.14　―ペイント・イット・ブラック―

**Phase-5 volume thesis:** The opening of the final arc tests moral and political recoverability. Justice shifts from aspiration toward dangerous identity-possession; No Face offers peace without free hearts; Lyudmila pressures Sirin role-fixity; distributed command succeeds; legal punishment coexists with nonforgiveness; the epilogue remains OPEN.

**Mandatory revision boundary:** Potential recovery is not guaranteed redemption; do not import V15+ outcomes into the throne/War Emperor epilogue.

## Source structure

- 序章　小部屋の中の爬虫ども（キャタピラーズ・イン・セル）
- 第一章　泥を這いずる（ワーム・イン・マッド）
- 第二章　黒蝶の落とし仔（スパングルズ・スパウン）
- 第三章　血黒の蝶（ブラッドブラック・ヴァネッサ）
- 第四章　白蝶瞬きて（フェイント・ホワイト）
- 第五章　羽化について（ネゴシエーション・オン・イマージェンス）
- 終章　緋虎は赫々と（クリムゾン・タイガー・バーニング）
- あとがき

## Evidence-responsibility checklist

- **Decisive events / causal reconstruction:** governed by the canonical V2 deep reading and the locked locator table below.
- **Character and relationship changes:** route to L01/L02 and T06/T09 where ordinary-life and relationship evidence is needed.
- **Institutions / politics:** route to L03 and T10/T11.
- **Military:** route claims through the Phase-5 military-level lock before specialist reuse.
- **Personhood / technology:** route to L05 and T02/T07.
- **Ethics / responsibility:** route through T08 plus the T14 claim-counterevidence lock.
- **Japanese voice / terminology:** T12 constraints are mandatory; English master terms must not erase lexical distinctions.
- **Motifs / visual form:** route to T13 and preserve the visual-evidence layer.
- **Later revisions:** use T14 plus the volume-specific revision boundary above.

## Relevant V14-boundary open questions

- **T14-OQ-01 - Federacy constitutional survival:** Does the Federacy remain a constitutional order after the reactionary break, rather than merely a surviving territory or military coalition?
- **T14-OQ-02 - New Imperial Court consent:** What political reality, consent, coercion, symbolism, or hostage pressure lies behind Frederica throne and Shin War Emperor positions?
- **T14-OQ-03 - Emergency powers:** Which V12-V14 restrictions expire, which are reviewed, and which harden into ordinary rule?
- **T14-OQ-04 - Political recoverability:** Can a polity institutionally return from fear and scapegoating without simply restoring an older regime or relying on one exceptional ruler?
- **T14-OQ-05 - Justice grammar:** Is the aspirational justice formula retained after its reactionary conversion into self-identification?
- **T14-OQ-09 - Fawn future:** Can Fawns be medically stabilized and civically integrated without either denial of danger or detention-as-disposal?
- **T14-OQ-10 - Artificial civic standing:** What legal status can Sirins, Fido-like subjects, Zelene, and other artificial subjects possess?
- **T14-OQ-11 - Lyudmila scope:** Is Lyudmila emergent fear an individual relationship effect, a repeatable Sirin capacity, or evidence of broader designed-subject revisability?
- **T14-OQ-12 - No Face end-revision:** Can No Face revise the terminal purpose organizing his agency, or is all sophistication inside a captured end?
- **T14-OQ-13 - No Face continuity:** What exact continuity connects Vaclav Milize to No Face?
- **T14-OQ-16 - Shin medical honesty:** Will Shin disclose, accept treatment for, and accommodate sensory loss when doing so threatens frontline usefulness?
- **T14-OQ-19 - Lena overresponsibility:** Can bounded responsibility survive direct personal catastrophe without renewed self-spending?
- **T14-OQ-25 - Frederica self-rule:** What does being her own king mean once inherited monarchy becomes politically active around her body?
- **T14-OQ-29 - War ending:** Can the war end without peace becoming another compulsory fixed social order?
- **T14-OQ-30 - Becoming after war:** What does becoming mean once crisis no longer forces repeated decisions and identities can stabilize for long periods?
- **T14-OQ-31 - Civic repair scale:** Can recipes, music, humor, archives, and education scale from reciprocal imagination into durable representation and law?
- **T14-OQ-32 - Eighty-Six identity after war:** Does Eighty-Six remain a chosen durable identity across citizenship, locality, family, and generational change?

# ALTER1 - Audited chronological supplement

**Authority:** supplemental only. Alter.1 can corroborate story-level chronology, low-pressure voice, ordinary-life baselines, birthdays/recurrence, archive recovery, and object/relationship history. It cannot silently override a contradiction in the numbered mainline volumes.

**Major controls:** ordinary humanity predates Giad; rescue adds infrastructure/reliability/options rather than creating humor or care; restored records do not manufacture home; recurring birthdays are evidence of repeatable future-time; dream/unconscious material must be separately source-graded.

# Global Phase-5 synthesis constraints

1. **T14 OPEN questions remain OPEN.** No specialist document may resolve them without new primary-source evidence.
2. **T12 lexical distinctions constrain English prose.** In particular, generic “return,” “home,” “personhood,” “support,” and “justice” require source-specific wording.
3. **Military claims name a level.** Tactical, operational, strategic, and political-constitutional claims must not be blended.
4. **Core-major characters require ordinary as well as climactic evidence.** Use the Phase-5 character matrix as a minimum evidentiary floor.
5. **Ethical claims carry counterevidence.** The T14 claim-counterevidence lock is a mandatory adversarial routing layer.
6. **Visual evidence is graded.** Persistent object, repeated prose motif, formal architecture, interior illustration, cover/front matter, and decorative/promotional graphics are not equivalent evidence classes.
7. **Frozen provenance is preserved.** Phase-2 packages/checksums remain untouched; this Phase-5 ledger is the current locator authority.
